# Stream State Change Report

Each section shows the full adjacent-frame score table for one raw ESP32 camera stream. A quiet run is any consecutive sequence of pairs whose `diffScore` stays below the stream-specific quiet threshold. The chosen bracket frame for a quiet run is the local minimum `quietScore` inside that run. Inline images are shown only for quiet pairs so the report stays focused on the comparable candidate blocks.

|---|---|
| Frames | 420 |
| Localization | score=1393.3, candidates=64, selected=64 |
| Thresholds (% changed board pixels) | center=28.21, mad=3.19, quiet=32.99, settle=37.77, motion=45.74 |
| Pixel diff cutoff | 2 |
| Quiet window | 2 frames |
| Quiet runs | 47 |
| Detected transition windows | 46 |
